Timing for Teams
Timing for Teams: Page title.
Timing for Teams lets your team share projects in the Mac app. Team management and viewing team members' times happen in the Timing web app. You can learn more [here](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsGeneral).
Timing for Teams: Description.
To start using Timing for Teams, you will need a Timing Sync account. [Click here](https://timingapp.com/syncPreferences) to sign up, then come back to this page.
Timing for Teams: Create sync account.
Please note that Timing for Teams requires a [subscription to our "Connect" plan](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsPricing). To get a free trial or find out about your upgrade options, please [contact us](TextFieldWithLinkSupport.support).
Timing for Teams: Subscription to higher tier required.
And to learn more about using Timing as a team manager, [have a look at this guide](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsAdmin).
Timing for Teams: Learn more about managing your teams.
Your team is ready to go!
Timing for Teams: Managing your teams (1).
Create and manage shared projects in the sidebar. To invite team members or view your team
s times, visit the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams).
Timing for Teams: Managing your teams (2).
You're set up as a team member!
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (1).
Assign time to your team
s shared projects in the sidebar, just like your personal projects. Your manager can only view the total time you spent on each team project in the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams), not which apps, documents, or websites you used.
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (2).
We have also prepared a short guide on using Timing as a team member [here](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsMember).
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (3).
Create a team or accept an invitation in the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams). Once your team is set up, shared projects will appear in the sidebar.
Timing for Teams: Create first team.
To learn more about how Timing for Teams works, [have a look at this guide](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsGeneral).
Timing for Teams: Learn more about teams (general).

Remove the MCP integration?
Preferences: Integrations: Alert to confirm removing the MCP integration (title).
This turns the MCP server off, so AI clients can no longer connect to Timing.
Authorized clients and their access keys are kept, and will work again if you add MCP back. To withdraw a client's access, use Revoke in Manage Authorized Clients before removing the integration.
Preferences: Integrations: Alert to confirm removing the MCP integration (message). Explains that removal disables the server but keeps authorized clients.

This will copy all selected projects and their sub-projects to this team. All corresponding app usage and time entries will be moved to the new projects.
Note that this migration should be done by only one team member; other team members can then manually drag their own app usage and time entries onto these projects instead.

Timing for Teams: Description.
To start using Timing for Teams, you will need a Timing Sync account. [Click here](https://timingapp.com/syncPreferences) to sign up, then come back to this page.
Timing for Teams: Create sync account.
Please note that Timing for Teams requires a [subscription to our "Connect" plan](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsPricing). To get a free trial or find out about your upgrade options, please [contact us](TextFieldWithLinkSupport.support).
Timing for Teams: Subscription to higher tier required.
And to learn more about using Timing as a team manager, [have a look at this guide](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsAdmin).
Timing for Teams: Learn more about managing your teams.
Your team is ready to go!
Timing for Teams: Managing your teams (1).
Create and manage shared projects in the sidebar. To invite team members or view your team
s times, visit the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams).
Timing for Teams: Managing your teams (2).
You're set up as a team member!
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (1).
Assign time to your team
s shared projects in the sidebar, just like your personal projects. Your manager can only view the total time you spent on each team project in the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams), not which apps, documents, or websites you used.
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (2).
We have also prepared a short guide on using Timing as a team member [here](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsMember).
Timing for Teams: How to use as a member (3).
Create a team or accept an invitation in the [Timing web app](https://web.timingapp.com/teams). Once your team is set up, shared projects will appear in the sidebar.
Timing for Teams: Create first team.
To learn more about how Timing for Teams works, [have a look at this guide](https://timingapp.com/appHelp/teamsGeneral).
Timing for Teams: Learn more about teams (general).

Remove the MCP integration?
Preferences: Integrations: Alert to confirm removing the MCP integration (title).
This turns the MCP server off, so AI clients can no longer connect to Timing.
Authorized clients and their access keys are kept, and will work again if you add MCP back. To withdraw a client's access, use Revoke in Manage Authorized Clients before removing the integration.
Preferences: Integrations: Alert to confirm removing the MCP integration (message). Explains that removal disables the server but keeps authorized clients.

This will copy all selected projects and their sub-projects to this team. All corresponding app usage and time entries will be moved to the new projects.
